# Existing transitional / anomaly classifiers and labels, keyed by collection and person

Built 2026-09-26 by `build_classifiers_table.py` (this folder) from the files named in section 1; second pass 2026-09-26 23:50-2026-09-27 00:30 added R25-R37 and the full acquisition denominators. Every cell is read from a file; nothing is a new call. The full join is `classifiers_by_person.csv` in this folder (22,173 rows, 226 columns; one row per (collection, person)). Section 3 prints the rows that carry at least one positive call or an outside read; section 4 the disagreements the rule classifier will have to adjudicate.

Vocabulary used in the compact columns (`norm()` in the script): `ordinary` (= registry `none`, COBRA `normal_lumbar_count`, VerSe 7/12/5/5), `L6`, `T13`, `four_lumbar`, `sacralised_fused` (= registry `sacralised_L5_fused`), `castellvi_I_II`, `lumbar_rib`, `eleven_rib` (VerSe 7/11/x/5, B `ribs:11`), `unassigned` (a four-lumbar count with no read), `unresolved` (the two labellers disagree), `no_count` (a junction reference outside the field: never ordinary), `review`.

## 0. What the SUPER DETECTOR RUN takes from this table

- **Human reads exist only on CTSP (33 records, two residents) and VerSe (325 subjects, published neuroradiologist table)**, plus three CTSpine1K list entries and the 70 externally labelled TotalSegmentator CTs (L6/T13 only). Every other collection (AbdomenAtlas, IDC, FDG-PET-CT, AMOS, COBRA, C-ArmLSpine, NLST, RibFrac, the rest) carries model calls only; the Figure 6 human worklist (172 COBRA/C-ArmLSpine persons) is unfilled.
- **The consolidated truth table is `labels_j3.csv` (R33)**: 321 read persons with LS/TL phenotypes; use it as the agreement reference, and the registry/screens (R9-R20) as the automated comparators.
- **Rule 1's second step already ran on CTSP (R30)**: 18 six-lumbar spines split A 11 / C 4 / B 1 / B-or-C 2; reproduce it per record.
- **T13 vs L6 on 25-presacral spines is a naming split (section 4 item 12)**: VERIDAH corrections, the VerSeFusion audit and TS v3's L6 class disagree with the VerSe read on the same bodies; score rule 1 and rule 3 against the read and report the split, do not average it away.
- **Stump rib and sacral shape have no validated existing classifier**: the Moller-mask derivation is 65.7 % sensitive (R27), the foramina counter fails on normals (R31). Arm B's Moller rib stage and the sacral-shape rule are new evidence, not a re-run.
- **Arm B's pelvis model: Dataset803 has no `results/` tree on the grid** (R36); pull the ensemble from HF `OpenSpineConsortium/spinopelvic-seg-checkpoints` before submitting (the grid HF cache held only `refs/main` at 00:04).

The BUU-LSPINE (144 film count anomalies), VinDr/LAT236 pseudolabels and FluoroCap captions from the anomaly manifest are film sources and are in the CSV under `buu_film`, `vindr_film_pseudo`, `lat236_film_pseudo`, `fluorocap_caption` for completeness only. R25-R37 were added in a second pass (2026-09-26 23:50): the first pass had left the Moller-mask stump flags, the VerSeFusion audit and VERIDAH corrections, the learned render flag, the TS v3 L6 class, the CTSP six-lumbar readings, the transition morphometrics, the expert re-read screen, the J3 label table, the Figure 6 worklist and the mask census out, and had joined only the emitted rows of the 17 acquisition screens; the full per-CT `rows.csv` (14,855 CTs) is now joined, so every screened CT appears with its A/B call, ordinary and no_count included.

Registry counts on `ctsp` are positives only (64 rows); the 738 CTSP records with no registry row are in the CSV through the manifest, the flag table, reader C and the frozen reference.

## 3. Per-person table: every person with a positive call from any source or an outside read

Columns: `read` = the outside read (CTSP consensus grade + worksheet count; VerSe table structure + Castellvi; TS external label; CTSpine1K list); `manifest` = CTSP six-way label; `registry` = subtype/tier; `subtypes` = the 2026-09-23 table; `A`, `B`, `C` = the three automated readers (registry A/B lumbosacral call, frozen-reference C; for acquisition collections the screen's A/B); `fusion` = Dawid-Skene call -> frozen assignment; `other` = COBRA id screen, TS12 verified, T13 rib check, VerSe mask ids, held-by-id-guard, screens, queues; `classes` = distinct positive classes across all sources (`positive_vs_ordinary_conflict` marks an ordinary call beside a positive one).

1048 rows (of 22,173); the film-source rows of the anomaly manifest are left to the CSV. Acquisition-screen rows whose subtype is `ordinary`, `no_count`, `unassigned` or `review` (a four-lumbar count or a labeller disagreement with no read; 1,283 + 430 in section 4) are in the CSV with their A/B calls (`acq_A_ls`, `acq_B_ls`) but are not printed here: the screen itself makes no positive call on them.

## 4. Where the sources disagree (the adjudication set for the rule classifier)

- persons with a positive call from one source and an `ordinary` call from another: **352** (cobra 46, ctsp 102, ctspine1k 14, ribfrac 1, ts 41, verse 148)
- persons carrying two or more different positive classes: **148** (carmlspine 3, cobra 12, ctsp 35, ctspine1k 2, ribfrac 1, ts 11, verse 84)
- registry `unresolved` / acquisition `review` (the two labellers disagree, no read): **1283** (abdomenatlas3_mini 669, amos22 44, aortaseg60 4, barts_crl 38, cobra 11, ctspine1k 15, curvas_pdacvi 11, deep_psma 5, idc 214, kidney_thoraxabdomen 29, quadra_hc 6, ribfrac 29, tcia_fdg_pet_ct_lesions 104, ts 74, waw_tace 30)
- `unassigned` (a four-lumbar count with no read: sacralised L5 vs numerical four-lumbar undecided): **430** (abdomenatlas3_mini 233, amos22 24, barts_crl 7, carmlspine 1, cobra 6, csi2014 2, ctpelvic1k_clinic 1, ctsp 5, curvas 1, deep_psma 1, idc 59, kidney_thoraxabdomen 4, quadra_hc 1, ribfrac 1, tcia_fdg_pet_ct_lesions 29, ts 26, verse 23, waw_tace 6)

Specific sets the rule classifier must not silently absorb:

1. **CTSP rib-convention 'L6' (rib-free 20th body, last ribs on T11, 24 presacral)**: 0105 0359 0474 0544 0552 0672 0895 1151 1153 (two-labeller) + 0090 0150 (label defects). Six rib-free bodies between the last rib and the sacrum, so Greg's rule 1 counts six; morphology of the sixth body (thoracic-type TPs) is exactly rule 1's second step. No reader has seen them; the plan calls them TL-11, never LS-L6. The VerSe reads 7/11/6/5 (verse544 581 606 626 636 642 765 814) are the same anatomy with a read.
2. **CTSP read count != release count**: 0008 0021 0033 0049 0176 0179 0196 ('6 (NA 4/5)'); 0176's readers wrote 'S1 lumbarization' and the sacral reformat shows a body-shaped segment inside the release sacrum.
3. **CTSP manifest 'SACRALIZATION' on read L6 spines**: 0005 (IV, count 6, 6 ids) and 0097 (IIb, count 6, 6 ids) carry `lstv_label=SACRALIZATION`; the manifest's pelvic-priority rule, not the count.
4. **0068**: adjudicated normal in the release, 203k-voxel L6 label, `auto_truth.csv` human-truth L6, registry two-labeller; unsettled.
5. **SPINEPS puts the CTSP read L6 inside its sacrum instance** on 7/18 (0097 0268 0376 looked at): rule 2's 'total sacralisation absorbs into the sacrum' is what labeller B does to a Castellvi II-IV L6, so the sacral-morphology branch has to separate an absorbed sixth lumbar-type body from a genuinely fused L5.
6. **VerSe reads contradicted by the scan**: verse026 512 540 571 612 765 814 (L6), verse278 507 615 (Castellvi II), verse588 601 (T13 by A), verse559 (four-lumbar), verse707 (normal read, id-25 mask).
7. **VerSe six-lumbar reads whose mask carries no id 25** (Castellvi III/IV unsegmented): 32-39 subjects; a count over mask ids gives five there.
8. **COBRA**: the mask-id screen's 38 L6 / 45 T13 / 8 four-lumbar against the counted registry (3 L6 two-/single-labeller, 6 four-lumbar, 11 unresolved, 497 no_count); centre G's 22 junction-only VERIDAH L6.
9. **TotalSegmentator**: 74 unresolved + 26 unassigned + 22 L6 + 4 T13, zero reads; the 70 externally labelled CTs are the only outside check (A misses 16/28 L6, B 11/28).
10. **T13 anywhere**: VerSe 6 read (4 uncontradicted); TS 4 (s0616 verified); RibFrac 4 single-labeller with the sacrum out of field ('T13 vs lumbar rib open'); NLST 5 kept of 34 named; COBRA 45 named, 0 countable; CTSP 0 countable (no FOV reaches T1) although VERIDAH names T13 on 52.
11. **Lumbar rib / hypoplastic 12th**: CTSP 14 release rows (9 two-labeller; 0720 and 1090 wrong), VerSe 12 read (13 scans; Moller masks 65.7 % sensitive), 18 VerSe hypoplastic-12th kept as `unassigned`; TS 39 short-lowest-rib + 87 short-bone candidates with no call (the signal marked 5/16 CTSP lumbar-rib spines and 26/746 ordinary ones).
12. **T13 vs L6 is a naming split on 25-presacral spines, and three sources take the other side from the VerSe read**: the VERIDAH corrections (R26) call T13 on 14 VerSe subjects the table reads L6 (11) or 11-rib (3); the VerSeFusion audit (R25) calls T13 on 18 of which 11 are read L6; TS v3's L6 class (R29) sits on s0616 (TS12-verified T13) and s1287 (validation screen T13). Rule 1 (count rib-free bodies between the last rib-bearing body and the sacrum) and rule 3 (13 rib pairs / stump rib + odd body) will split these by whether the 20th body bears a rib, which is exactly what the sources disagree on.
13. **CTSP six-lumbar spines already run through rule 1's morphology step (R30)**: A true L6 11, C lumbarised S1 4 (0033 0097 0234 0486), B aplastic-rib T12 1 (0068), B-or-C 2 (0376 1053); the shift test says B for 0068 0376 0950 1053 while readers counted six lumbar-type bodies on 17 of the 18. The new run should reproduce or overturn these per record, not start blind.
14. **TotalSegmentator external L6 vs TS v3 L6 vs the registry**: 19 of 28 external L6 carry a v3 L6; the 9 without (s0071 s0086 s0158 s0366 s0442 s0620 s0683 s1022 s1088) and the registry's ordinary on s1224 (external L6) are the TS adjudication set.
15. **Stump rib**: the Moller-mask derivation (R27) and the VerSe table disagree on 45 subjects (flag 0 on 40 read stumps, flag 1 on 5 read none); arm B's Moller rib stage replaces this derivation, and these 45 are its first check.
16. **Sacral shape**: no validated sacral-shape classifier exists on disk. The foramina counter (R31) fails the four-pairs check on normals; the mask census (R35) describes representation only; `six_sacral_type_score` (R30) is the only per-record sacral-type number and it is defined on 18 records.

## 5. Files

- `classifiers_by_person.csv`: the full join (this folder).
- `build_classifiers_table.py`: regenerates both files.
- `grid_pull/`: copies of the grid-only inputs (anomaly manifest, spine classes, the 17 acquisition `registry_rows.csv` in `acq/` and full per-CT `rows.csv` in `acq_rows/`, `lstv_flag_disagreements.csv`, `lstv_flag_crosstab.txt`; `versefusion/` audit, VERIDAH corrections, disputed twelve, T13 adjudication; `lstv_flag/` Moller stump table, render-flag OOF scores, lumbar-rib sheet, TRANSITIONAL_LABELS.md; `ts/` v3 L6 probe, lumbar-rib candidates; `mask_census.json`; `ds813/dataset.json`).
- `stats_new.py`: the cross-tabulations quoted in R25-R37 and section 4 items 12-16.
